What this means:
A Google News RSS item pairs a DRAM shortage with rising auto-industry component prices, with no ticker or numeric figure attached.

内容:
一则 Google 新闻 RSS 报道称 DRAM 短缺已波及汽车行业，导致零部件价格上涨，未提供具体 ticker 或数字。

Why it matters:
Memory/component shortages that spill into unrelated end-markets like autos signal broad-based supply tightness rather than an AI-datacenter-specific squeeze, which weakens the case that this is purely an AI-capex bubble tell — but persistent DRAM/HBM tightness still feeds hyperscaler cost pressure either way.

影响:
内存/元器件短缺蔓延到汽车这类非 AI 数据中心的下游市场，说明这更像是广泛的供应紧张，而非单纯针对 AI 数据中心的挤压，这在一定程度上削弱了其作为纯 AI capex 泡沫信号的说服力——但持续的 DRAM/HBM 紧张仍会传导为超大规模厂商的成本压力。

内存价格紧张（NAND 短缺）与 AI capex 驱动的需求（Google、Vera Rubin）同时出现，正是 C6 信号要捕捉的模式——超大规模厂商的 AI 建设已经明显挤压上游内存供应，这种成本压力可能传导到所有 AI capex 预算中。

MED C10 [C10-MED] 10y Real Yield rising: 2.39% (+56bp off 90d-low) 2026-07-23 22:29 UTC

Date:     2026-07-22
Current:  2.39%
90d low:  1.83%
Move:     +56bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The 10-year real (inflation-adjusted) Treasury yield has risen ≥40 bp off its recent trailing low.

Why it matters:
Real yields are the discount rate on every long-duration cash flow — and AI-infra valuations are the longest-duration bet in the market. A real-yield spike compresses those valuations directly and raises the true cost of the debt funding the buildout, regardless of where headline rates sit. This is the cleanest read on money getting genuinely more expensive.

MED C10 [C10-MED] 10y Real Yield rising: 2.37% (+54bp off 90d-low) 2026-07-22 22:29 UTC 
Series:   REAL10 (DFII10)
Date:     2026-07-21
Current:  2.37%
90d low:  1.83%
Move:     +54bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The 10-year real (inflation-adjusted) Treasury yield has risen ≥40 bp off its recent trailing low.

Why it matters:
Real yields are the discount rate on every long-duration cash flow — and AI-infra valuations are the longest-duration bet in the market. A real-yield spike compresses those valuations directly and raises the true cost of the debt funding the buildout, regardless of where headline rates sit. This is the cleanest read on money getting genuinely more expensive.

MED C10 [C10-MED] 10y Real Yield rising: 2.35% (+52bp off 90d-low) 2026-07-21 22:29 UTC 
Series:   REAL10 (DFII10)
Date:     2026-07-20
Current:  2.35%
90d low:  1.83%
Move:     +52bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The 10-year real (inflation-adjusted) Treasury yield has risen ≥40 bp off its recent trailing low.

Why it matters:
Real yields are the discount rate on every long-duration cash flow — and AI-infra valuations are the longest-duration bet in the market. A real-yield spike compresses those valuations directly and raises the true cost of the debt funding the buildout, regardless of where headline rates sit. This is the cleanest read on money getting genuinely more expensive.

MED C10 [C10-MED] 10y Real Yield rising: 2.31% (+48bp off 90d-low) 2026-07-20 22:29 UTC 
Series:   REAL10 (DFII10)
Date:     2026-07-17
Current:  2.31%
90d low:  1.83%
Move:     +48bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The 10-year real (inflation-adjusted) Treasury yield has risen ≥40 bp off its recent trailing low.

Why it matters:
Real yields are the discount rate on every long-duration cash flow — and AI-infra valuations are the longest-duration bet in the market. A real-yield spike compresses those valuations directly and raises the true cost of the debt funding the buildout, regardless of where headline rates sit. This is the cleanest read on money getting genuinely more expensive.
